Он не может быстро всплыть или же в процессе всплытия не сможет обеспечить энергией защитные системы, и его уничтожат в процессе, а повреждения систем связи и привели к тому, что мы имеем сейчас - невозможность связаться с Кланом напрямую, а широкополосная подача сигнала. Либо эта технология является резервной, возможно, крайней степени кустарного изготовления, либо повреждения критические, и потому при пробитии межизмеренческих мембран сигнал искажается настолько, что частота излучения падает до уровня радио.

            Tranclucator ^{
                ConstructionDoneMsg=<color=255,240,100>Донесение из штаба:</color>
                ConstructionDoneMsg=Мы закончили сборку <color=255,240,100>транклюкатора</color>, босс. Новый механический друг с завидным нетерпением ожидает тебя на складе.
                Cost=-1
                CostCalc ^{
                    01=dword seed = Id(GetShipRuins(Player())) + CurTurn() * CurTurn();
                    02=int result_cost = max(Rnd(GalaxyMoney(2, 4) - 500, GalaxyMoney(2, 4) + 500, seed), 1000);
                    03=float TL_coef = -4.0 + GalaxyTechLevel();
                    04=if(TL_coef < 0) TL_coef = 0;
                    05=TL_coef = 0.5 * TL_coef + 1.0;
                    06=float Diff_coef = 0.85 + 0.15 * max(GalaxyDiffLevels(1) / 50 - 1, 1);
                    07=result_cost = round(TL_coef * Diff_coef * result_cost);
                    08=result_cost = result_cost + Rnd(-50, 100, seed);
                    09=if(result_cost <= 0) result_cost = 1;
                }
                DelayInProduction=365
                DelayInProductionAddText=[станет доступно через <NextProductionDaysLeft> дн.]
                Description=Проект: <clr>Транклюкатор<clrEnd>
                Description=Описание: Стандартный транклюкатор класса "Долбобот", какие в качестве автономной рабочей силы использовал ещё мой прадедушка. Уж больно тупые.

    ShuMercsHQ_GluonizingEnergy ^{
        00=[t_OnWeaponShot|]
        01=dword target = ScriptItemActObject1();
        02=if(ObjectType(target) > 5) exit;
        03=if(~ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(target, 'ShuMercsHQ_GluonizationEffect'))
        03 ^{
            01=int effect_strength = ShipCustomShipInfoData(target, 'ShuMercsHQ_GluonizationEffect', 1) + 1;
            02=ScriptItemActParam(round((1.0 + min(0.10 * effect_strength, 6.5)) * ScriptItemActParam()));
            03=ShipCustomShipInfoData(target, 'ShuMercsHQ_GluonizationEffect', 1, effect_strength);
        }
        04=else
        04 ^{
            01=ShipAddCustomShipInfo(target, 'ShuMercsHQ_GluonizationEffect', 'NoShow', 1);
        }
    }
    ShuMercsHQ_GluonizingMissile ^{
        00=[t_OnMissileShot2|]
        01=dword target = ScriptItemActObject1();
        02=if(ObjectType(target) > 5) exit;
        03=if(~ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(target, 'ShuMercsHQ_GluonizationEffect'))
        03 ^{

            04=MissileMaxDamage(missile, round((1.0 + min(0.033 * effect_strength, 6.5)) * MissileMaxDamage(missile)));
            05=ShipCustomShipInfoData(target, 'ShuMercsHQ_GluonizationEffect', 1, effect_strength);
        }
        04=else ShipAddCustomShipInfo(target, 'ShuMercsHQ_GluonizationEffect', 'NoShow', 1);
    }

                OnActCode ^{
                    01=dword cur_info = CurInfo();
                    02=dword cur_ship = ScriptItemActShip();

                    05=if(obj_type != ShipCustomShipInfoData(0, cur_info, 1)) exit;

                    06 ^{

                        01 ^{
                            01=int num_type = 0;
                            02=if(obj_type == 4 || obj_type == 5) num_type = ShipTypeN(dealer);
                            03=else if(obj_type == 6) num_type = ItemType(dealer);
                            04=if(num_type != ShipCustomShipInfoData(0, cur_info, 2)) exit;
                        }

                        02 ^{
                            01=int obj_Id = 0;
                            02=if((obj_type >= 4 && obj_type <= 7) || obj_type == 1) obj_Id = Id(dealer);
                            03=if(obj_Id != ShipCustomShipInfoData(0, cur_info, 3)) exit;
                        }
                        03=str text_filt_1 = ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(0, cur_info, 1);
                        04=if(text_filt_1 != '')
                        04 ^{
                            01=str match_str = '';
                            02=if(obj_type == 6) match_str = EquipmentImageName(dealer);
                            03=if(match_str != text_filt_1) exit;
                        }
                    }
                    07=ScriptItemActParam(ScriptItemActParam() + HullHP(cur_ship));
                }

            }

                        04 ^{

                            02=float percent = ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(0, cur_info, 2);
                            03=new_exp = round(percent * new_exp);
                            04=ShipFreeExp(cur_ship, ShipFreeExp(cur_ship) + new_exp);
                            05=SetShipExpByType(cur_ship, GetShipExpByType(cur_ship) + new_exp, 0);
                            06=ShipCustomShipInfoData(0, cur_info, 3, cur_exp);
                        }

                        06=int artBioCheck = ArtefactTypeInUse(Player(), t_ArtBio);
                        07=if(artBioCheck)
                        07 ^{
                            01=if(RndObject(1, 100, cur_ship) <= 25 * artBioCheck) ShipCustomShipInfoData(0, cur_info, 1, end_date + 1);
                        }
                        08=if(end_date <= CurTurn())
                        08 ^{
                            01=str bons = ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(0, cur_info, 1);
                            02=for(int i = ParsCountFromString(bons, ',') - 1; i >= 0; i = i - 1)
                            02 ^{
                                01=str bon_str = GetParFromString(bons, ',', i, 0);
                                02=int bon = GetValueFromScript('', GetParFromString(bon_str, '=', 0, 0));
                                03=ShipSpecialBonuses(cur_ship, bon, ShipSpecialBonuses(cur_ship, bon) - GetParFromString(bon_str, '=', 1, 0));
                            }
                            03=ShipDeleteCustomShipInfo(cur_ship, 'ShuMercsHQ_ExpirinStimulant');
                            04=if(!ScriptItemActObject1()) Ether(0, '', Format(CT('Illness.Stimulant.Expirin.End'), '<Date'+'>', GameDateTxtByTurn(CurTurn()), '255,240,100'));
                        }
                    }
                    03=else
                    03 ^{
                        01=artBioCheck = ArtefactTypeInUse(Player(), t_ArtBio);
                        02=if(!artBioCheck)
                        02 ^{
                            01=if(ArtefactTypeInUse(Player(), t_ArtArtefactor))
                            01 ^{

                                            02=break;
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        03=if(!artBioCheck) ShipCustomShipInfoDescription(0, cur_info, ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(0, cur_info, 3));

                    }
                }

                OnActStepTypes=11

                            02=for(int j = 1; j <= ShipCntWeapon(cur_ruin); j = j + 1)
                            03 ^{
                                01=dword cur_gun = ShipEqInSlot(cur_ruin, t_Weapon1, j);
                                02=if(MissilesToRearm(cur_gun)) MissilesToRearm(cur_gun, MissilesToRearm(cur_gun));
                            }
                            04=int perm = 0;
                            05=if(ShipType(cur_ruin) == 'PlayerMercHQ_licensed') perm = 1;
                            06=for(j = 0; j < ShipItems(cur_ruin); j = j + 1)
                            07 ^{
                                01=dword cur_item = ShipItems(cur_ruin, j);
                                02=if(!ItemIsInUse(cur_item)) continue;
                                03=int item_type = ItemType(cur_item);
                                04=if(item_type < t_Hull || item_type > t_CustomWeapon) continue;
                                05=if(ItemDurability(cur_item) < 100)
                                06 ^{
                                    01=if(item_type <= t_DefGenerator) ItemDurability(cur_item, 100);
                                    02=else
                                    03 ^{
                                        01=if(item_type >= t_Weapon13 && item_type <= t_Weapon15)
                                        02 ^{
                                            01=if(perm) ItemDurability(cur_item, 100);
                                        }
                                        03=else if(item_type == t_CustomWeapon)
                                        04 ^{
                                            01=if(GetCustomWeaponData(CustomWeaponType(cur_item), 'Availability') == 'NotSoldAndNodeRepair')
                                            02 ^{
                                                01=if(perm) ItemDurability(cur_item, 100);
                                            }
                                            03=else ItemDurability(cur_item, 100);
                                        }
                                        05=else ItemDurability(cur_item, 100);
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            08=if(perm)
                            09 ^{
                                01=for(j = 0; j < ShipArts(cur_ruin); j = j + 1)
                                02 ^{
                                    01=cur_item = ShipArts(cur_ruin, j);
                                    02=if(!ItemIsInUse(cur_item)) continue;
                                    03=if(ItemDurability(cur_item) < 100) ItemDurability(cur_item, 100);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                OnActCodeTypes=t_OnStep
                OnActStepTypes=11
            }

            ShuMercsHQ_StrikeGroupMercInfo ^{

                Icon=0

                            03=else PlayerHQMainArray['Mercs', 'StrikeCandidatesNamesKIALine'] = PlayerHQMainArray['Mercs', 'StrikeCandidatesNamesKIALine'] + ',' + merc_name;
                        }

                    02=else
                    02 ^{
                        01=if(CurrentForm() == 'Ship')
                        02 ^{
                            01=dword cur_info = CurInfo();
                            02=cur_ship = FormCurShip();
                            03=dword cur_place = GetShipRuins(cur_ship);
                            04=if(!cur_place) cur_place = GetShipPlanet(cur_ship);
                            05=int bon_num = FindBonusByNameInCfg('ShuMercsHQ_StrikeMercItemCostMark');
                            06=if(!fun_init)
                            07 ^{
                                01=unknown RndObject = ImportedFunction('UtilityFunctions', 'RndObject');
                                02=unknown GetParFromString = ImportedFunction('UtilityFunctions', 'GetParFromString');
                                03=int fun_init = 1;
                            }
                            08=if(ScriptItemActionType(t_OnEnteringForm))
                            09 ^{
                                01=for(int i = ShipItems(cur_ship) - 1; i >= 0; i = i - 1)
                                02 ^{
                                    01=dword item = ShipItems(cur_ship, i);
                                    02=if(!i)
                                    02 ^{
                                        01=if(ShipType(cur_ship) == 'PlayerMerc')
                                        02 ^{
                                            01=NoDropItem(item, 0);
                                            02=if(ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, FindBonusByNameInCfg('ShuMercsHQ_StrikeMercOwnItemMark')))
                                            03 ^{
                                                01=ItemExtraSpecialsAddByType(item, bon_num, ItemCost(item));
                                                02=ItemCost(item, 0);
                                            }
                                            04=continue;
                                        }
                                        03=continue;
                                    }
                                    03=if(ItemType(item) == t_UselessItem)
                                    03 ^{
                                        01=if(EquipmentImageName(item) == 'WeightReserve') FreeItem(GetItemFromShip(cur_ship, item));
                                        02=continue;
                                    }
                                    04=if(ItemType(item) > t_CustomWeapon) continue;
                                    05=if(NoDropItem(item))
                                    05 ^{
                                        01=NoDropItem(item, 0);
                                        02=continue;
                                    }
                                    06=ItemExtraSpecialsAddByType(item, bon_num, ItemCost(item));
                                    07=ItemCost(item, 0);
                                }
                                03=for(i = ShipArts(cur_ship) - 1; i >= 0; i = i - 1)
                                04 ^{
                                    01=item = ShipArts(cur_ship, i);
                                    02=if(NoDropItem(item))
                                    03 ^{
                                        01=NoDropItem(item, 0);
                                        02=continue;
                                    }
                                    04=ItemExtraSpecialsAddByType(item, bon_num, ItemCost(item));
                                    05=ItemCost(item, 0);
                                }
                                05=if(IsScriptActive('Mod_EvoArmada'))
                                06 ^{
                                    01=int attacks_count = ShipSpecialBonuses(cur_ship, bonAttacks);
                                    02=if(attacks_count > 0)
                                    03 ^{
                                        01=for(i = 1; i <= 5; i = i + 1)
                                        02 ^{
                                            01=if(ShipEqInSlot(cur_ship, t_Weapon1, i))
                                            02 ^{
                                                01=InterfaceText('Ship', 'GunBatteryLabelSlot' + i, 'x' + (attacks_count + 1));
                                                03=InterfaceState('Ship', 'GunBatteryLabelSlot' + i, 1);
                                            }
                                            03=else
                                            04 ^{
                                                01=InterfaceState('Ship', 'GunBatteryLabelSlot' + i, 0);
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                    04=else
                                    05 ^{
                                        01=for(i = 1; i <= 5; i = i + 1)
                                        02 ^{
                                            01=InterfaceState('Ship', 'GunBatteryLabelSlot' + i, 0);
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            10=else if(ScriptItemActionType(t_OnReEnteringForm))
                            11 ^{
                                01=if(!ShipEqInSlot(cur_ship, t_Engine))
                                02 ^{
                                    01=if(FormShipCurItem('MoveType'))
                                    02 ^{
                                        01=FormShipCurItem('PutBack');
                                    }
                                    03=else
                                    04 ^{
                                        01=for(i = 0; i < StorageItems(); i = i + 1)
                                        02 ^{
                                            01=if(StorageItemLocation(i) != cur_place) continue;
                                            02=item = StorageItems(i);
                                            03=if(ItemType(item) != t_Engine) continue;
                                            04=if(!ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num)) continue;
                                            05=AddItemToShip(cur_ship, GetItemFromStorage(i));
                                            06=ItemIsInUse(item, cur_ship, 1);
                                            07=break;
                                        }
                                        03=if(!ShipEqInSlot(cur_ship, t_Engine))
                                        04 ^{
                                            01=for(i = 0; i < ShopItems(cur_place); i = i + 1)
                                            02 ^{
                                                01=item = ShopItems(cur_place, i);
                                                02=if(ItemType(item) != t_Engine) continue;
                                                03=if(!ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num)) continue;
                                                04=AddItemToShip(cur_ship, GetItemFromShop(cur_place, item));
                                                05=ItemIsInUse(item, cur_ship, 1);
                                                06=break;
                                            }
                                        }
                                        05=if(!ShipEqInSlot(cur_ship, t_Engine))
                                        06 ^{
                                            01=dword last_item = 0;
                                            02=for(i = 1; i < ShipItems(cur_ship); i = i + 1)
                                            03 ^{
                                                01=item = ShipItems(cur_ship, i);
                                                02=if(ItemType(item) != t_Engine) continue;
                                                03=if(!ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num)) continue;
                                                04=last_item = item;
                                            }
                                            04=if(last_item) ItemIsInUse(last_item, cur_ship, 1);
                                        }
                                    }
                                    05=Sound('Sound.NoSize');
                                    06=if(ShipType(cur_ship) == 'PlayerMerc')
                                    06 ^{

                                    }
                                    07=else MessageBox(CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.DontTakeAwayMyFuelTankBoss'), 10);
                                    08=exit;
                                }

                                06 ^{

                                    02 ^{
                                        01=FormShipCurItem('PutBack');
                                    }
                                    03=else
                                    04 ^{
                                        01=for(i = 0; i < StorageItems(); i = i + 1)
                                        02 ^{
                                            01=if(StorageItemLocation(i) != cur_place) continue;
                                            02=item = StorageItems(i);
                                            03=if(ItemType(item) < t_Weapon1) continue;
                                            04=if(!ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num)) continue;
                                            05=AddItemToShip(cur_ship, GetItemFromStorage(i));
                                            06=ItemIsInUse(item, cur_ship, 1);
                                            07=break;
                                        }
                                        03=if(!ShipCntWeapon(cur_ship))
                                        04 ^{
                                            01=for(i = 0; i < ShopItems(cur_place); i = i + 1)
                                            02 ^{
                                                01=item = ShopItems(cur_place, i);
                                                02=if(ItemType(item) < t_Weapon1) continue;
                                                03=if(!ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num)) continue;
                                                04=AddItemToShip(cur_ship, GetItemFromShop(cur_place, item));
                                                05=ItemIsInUse(item, cur_ship, 1);
                                                06=break;
                                            }
                                        }
                                        05=if(!ShipCntWeapon(cur_ship))
                                        06 ^{
                                            01=last_item = 0;
                                            02=for(i = 1; i < ShipItems(cur_ship); i = i + 1)
                                            03 ^{
                                                01=item = ShipItems(cur_ship, i);
                                                02=if(ItemType(item) < t_Weapon1) continue;
                                                03=if(!ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num)) continue;
                                                04=last_item = item;
                                            }
                                            04=if(last_item) ItemIsInUse(last_item, cur_ship, 1);
                                        }
                                    }
                                    05=Sound('Sound.NoSize');
                                    06=if(ShipType(cur_ship) == 'PlayerMerc')
                                    06 ^{
                                        01=phrases_path = 'Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.CandidatesList.Class' + substr(GetData(0, cur_ship), 0, 1) + '.' + substr(GetData(0, cur_ship), 1) + '.PersonalPhrases';
                                        02=if(BlockExist(phrases_path)) MessageBox(CT(phrases_path + '.DontTakeAwayMyLastGunBoss'), 10);
                                        03=else MessageBox(CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.DontTakeAwayMyLastGunBoss'), 10);
                                    }
                                    07=else MessageBox(CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.DontTakeAwayMyLastGunBoss'), 10);
                                    08=exit;
                                }
                                07=int tranc_message_showed = 0;
                                08=for(i = ShipArts(cur_ship) - 1; i >= 0; i = i - 1)
                                09 ^{
                                    01=item = ShipArts(cur_ship, i);
                                    02=if(ItemType(item) == t_ArtefactTranclucator)
                                    03 ^{
                                        01=if(GetMainData('Data.NPCTrancOwners.Enabled') == 'True')
                                        02 ^{
                                            01=int tranc_infos = ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(cur_ship, 'TrancNPCOwnerInfo');
                                            02=dword tranc = ArtTranclucatorToShip(item);
                                            03=if(~tranc_infos)
                                            04 ^{
                                                01=str allId = ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 1);
                                                02=int quant = ShipCustomShipInfoData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 3);
                                                03=int is_new_tranc = 1;
                                                04=dword trancId = Id(tranc);
                                                05=for(int j = 0; j < quant; j = j + 1)
                                                06 ^{
                                                    01=dword tempId = GetParFromString(allId, ',', j, 0);
                                                    02=if(trancId == tempId)
                                                    03 ^{
                                                        01=is_new_tranc = 0;
                                                        02=break;
                                                    }
                                                }
                                                07=if(is_new_tranc)
                                                08 ^{
                                                    01=if(quant < 6 && ShipSkill(cur_ship, 'Technical') >= quant + 1)
                                                    02 ^{
                                                        01=str launched = ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 2);
                                                        02=str status = ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 3);
                                                        03=if(ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(tranc, 'TrancNPCAddInfo') == -1) ShipAddCustomShipInfo(tranc, 'TrancNPCAddInfo', 'NoShow', -1, 0, 0, '', '0', 'U');
                                                        04=if(~ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(tranc, 'TrancModAddInfo')) ShipDeleteCustomShipInfo(tranc, 'TrancModAddInfo');
                                                        05=if(~ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(tranc, 'TrancNavComputerInfo'))
                                                        06 ^{
                                                            01=ShipDeleteCustomShipInfo(tranc, 'TrancNavComputerInfo');
                                                            02=ShipAddCustomShipInfo(tranc, 'TrancFakeNavComputerInfo');
                                                        }
                                                        07=quant = quant + 1;
                                                        08=ShipCustomShipInfoData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 3, quant);
                                                        09=allId = allId + ',' + Id(tranc);
                                                        10=ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 1, allId);
                                                        11=launched = launched + 'N';
                                                        12=ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 2, launched);
                                                        13=status = status + 'U';
                                                        14=ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 3, status);
                                                        15=TranclucatorData(tranc, 'Proprietor', cur_ship);
                                                        16=TranclucatorData(tranc, 'AutoArrange', 1);
                                                    }
                                                    03=else
                                                    04 ^{
                                                        01=AddItemToStorage(cur_place, GetArtFromShip(cur_ship, item));
                                                        02=if(quant < 6)
                                                        03 ^{
                                                            01=if(!tranc_message_showed)
                                                            02 ^{
                                                                01=if(ShipType(cur_ship) == 'PlayerMerc')
                                                                01 ^{
                                                                    01=phrases_path = 'Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.CandidatesList.Class' + substr(GetData(0, cur_ship), 0, 1) + '.' + substr(GetData(0, cur_ship), 1) + '.PersonalPhrases';
                                                                    02=if(BlockExist(phrases_path)) MessageBox(CT(phrases_path + '.NotEnoughTechSkillForTrancsBoss'), 10);
                                                                    03=else MessageBox(CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.NotEnoughTechSkillForTrancsBoss'), 10);
                                                                }
                                                                02=else MessageBox(CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.NotEnoughTechSkillForTrancsBoss'), 10);
                                                                03=tranc_message_showed = 1;
                                                            }
                                                        }
                                                        04=else
                                                        05 ^{
                                                            01=if(!tranc_message_showed)
                                                            02 ^{
                                                                01=if(ShipType(cur_ship) == 'PlayerMerc')
                                                                01 ^{
                                                                    01=phrases_path = 'Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.CandidatesList.Class' + substr(GetData(0, cur_ship), 0, 1) + '.' + substr(GetData(0, cur_ship), 1) + '.PersonalPhrases';
                                                                    02=if(BlockExist(phrases_path)) MessageBox(CT(phrases_path + '.IHaveMaxTrancsBoss'), 10);
                                                                    03=else MessageBox(CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.IHaveMaxTrancsBoss'), 10);
                                                                }
                                                                02=else MessageBox(CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.IHaveMaxTrancsBoss'), 10);
                                                                03=tranc_message_showed = 1;
                                                            }
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            }
                                            05=else
                                            06 ^{
                                                01=if(ShipSkill(cur_ship, 'Technical') >= 1)
                                                02 ^{
                                                    01=tranc = ArtTranclucatorToShip(item);
                                                    02=if(ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(tranc, 'TrancNPCAddInfo') == -1) ShipAddCustomShipInfo(tranc, 'TrancNPCAddInfo', 'NoShow', -1, 0, 0, '', '0', 'U');
                                                    03=if(~ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(tranc, 'TrancModAddInfo')) ShipDeleteCustomShipInfo(tranc, 'TrancModAddInfo');
                                                    04=if(~ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(tranc, 'TrancNavComputerInfo'))
                                                    05 ^{
                                                        01=ShipDeleteCustomShipInfo(tranc, 'TrancNavComputerInfo');
                                                        02=ShipAddCustomShipInfo(tranc, 'TrancFakeNavComputerInfo');
                                                    }
                                                    06=ShipAddCustomShipInfo(cur_ship, 'TrancNPCOwnerInfo', 'NoShow', Rnd(207, 459), 1, 1, Id(tranc), 'N', 'U');
                                                }
                                                03=else
                                                04 ^{
                                                    01=AddItemToStorage(cur_place, GetArtFromShip(cur_ship, item));
                                                    02=if(!tranc_message_showed)
                                                    03 ^{
                                                        01=if(ShipType(cur_ship) == 'PlayerMerc')
                                                        01 ^{
                                                            01=phrases_path = 'Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.CandidatesList.Class' + substr(GetData(0, cur_ship), 0, 1) + '.' + substr(GetData(0, cur_ship), 1) + '.PersonalPhrases';
                                                            02=if(BlockExist(phrases_path)) MessageBox(CT(phrases_path + '.NotEnoughTechSkillForTrancsBoss'), 10);
                                                            03=else MessageBox(CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.NotEnoughTechSkillForTrancsBoss'), 10);
                                                        }
                                                        02=else MessageBox(CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.PlayerMercsStrikeGroup.NotEnoughTechSkillForTrancsBoss'), 10);
                                                        03=tranc_message_showed = 1;
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                10=for(i = StorageItems() - 1; i >= 0 ; i = i - 1)
                                11 ^{
                                    01=if(StorageItemLocation(i) != cur_place) continue;

                                    03 ^{
                                        01=if(GetMainData('Data.NPCTrancOwners.Enabled') == 'True')

                                            03 ^{
                                                01=allId = ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 1);

                                                03=quant = ShipCustomShipInfoData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 3);
                                                04=int deleted_tranc_num = -1;

                                                06=for(j = 0; j < quant; j = j + 1)
                                                07 ^{
                                                    01=tempId = GetParFromString(allId, ',', j, 0);
                                                    02=if(trancId == tempId) deleted_tranc_num = j;

                                                            04=ShipCustomShipInfoTextData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 3, status);
                                                        }
                                                        03=else ArrayAdd(tarr, GetParFromString(allId, ',', j, 0));
                                                    }
                                                    06=quant = quant - 1;
                                                    07=ShipCustomShipInfoData(cur_ship, tranc_infos, 3, quant);
                                                    08=allId = '';

                                                    10 ^{
                                                        01=if(j < quant) allId = allId + tarr[j] + ',';
                                                        02=else allId = allId + tarr[j];
                                                    }

                                                        02=ShipAddCustomShipInfo(tranc, 'TrancModAddInfo', 'NoShow', 0, 0);

                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                    04=int cost = ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num);
                                    05=if(!cost) continue;

                                    }
                                    07=else
                                    07 ^{
                                        01=if(ItemType(item) != t_Hull) AddItemToShip(cur_ship, GetItemFromStorage(i));
                                        02=else

                                            01=AddItemToStorage(cur_place, GetItemFromShip(cur_ship, ShipItems(cur_ship, 0)));
                                            02=AddItemToShip(cur_ship, GetItemFromStorage(i));
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                12=for(i = 0; i < ShopItems(cur_place); i = i + 1)
                                13 ^{
                                    01=item = ShopItems(cur_place, i);
                                    02=cost = ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num);
                                    03=if(!cost) continue;
                                    04=ItemCost(item, cost);
                                    05=ItemExtraSpecialsDeleteByType(item, bon_num, cost);
                                }
                                14=if(IsScriptActive('Mod_EvoArmada'))
                                15 ^{
                                    01=attacks_count = ShipSpecialBonuses(cur_ship, bonAttacks);
                                    02=if(attacks_count > 0)
                                    03 ^{
                                        01=for(i = 1; i <= 5; i = i + 1)
                                        02 ^{

                                            02 ^{

                                                03=InterfaceState('Ship', 'GunBatteryLabelSlot' + i, 1);
                                            }
                                            03=else
                                            04 ^{
                                                01=InterfaceState('Ship', 'GunBatteryLabelSlot' + i, 0);
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                    04=else
                                    05 ^{
                                        01=for(i = 1; i <= 5; i = i + 1)
                                        02 ^{
                                            01=InterfaceState('Ship', 'GunBatteryLabelSlot' + i, 0);
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            12=else
                            13 ^{
                                01=for(i = 0; i < ShipItems(cur_ship); i = i + 1)
                                02 ^{
                                    01=item = ShipItems(cur_ship, i);
                                    02=cost = ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num);
                                    03=if(!i)
                                    04 ^{
                                        01=NoDropItem(item, 1);
                                        02=if(cost)
                                        03 ^{
                                            01=ItemCost(item, cost);
                                            02=ItemExtraSpecialsDeleteByType(item, bon_num, cost);
                                        }
                                        04=continue;
                                    }
                                    05=if(!cost)
                                    06 ^{
                                        01=NoDropItem(item, 1);
                                        02=continue;
                                    }
                                    07=ItemCost(item, cost);
                                    08=ItemExtraSpecialsDeleteByType(item, bon_num, cost);
                                }
                                03=for(i = 0; i < ShipArts(cur_ship); i = i + 1)
                                04 ^{
                                    01=item = ShipArts(cur_ship, i);
                                    02=cost = ItemExtraSpecialsCountByType(item, bon_num);
                                    03=if(!cost)
                                    04 ^{
                                        01=NoDropItem(item, 1);
                                        02=continue;
                                    }
                                    05=ItemCost(item, cost);
                                    06=ItemExtraSpecialsDeleteByType(item, bon_num, cost);
                                }
                                05=if(~ShipFindCustomShipInfoByType(cur_ship, 'TrancNPCOwnerInfo'))
                                06 ^{
                                    08=item = CreateQuestItem('WeightReserve', 0);
                                    09=ItemSize(item, 0);
                                    10=AddItemToShip(cur_ship, item);
                                    11=NoDropItem(item, 1);
                                    12=ItemIsInUse(item, cur_ship, 1);
                                }
                                07=ArrangeItems(cur_ship);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                OnActCodeTypes=t_OnEnteringForm,t_OnReEnteringForm,t_OnLeavingForm,t_OnDeath
            }
        }
    }

    ShuMercsHQ_HoloTablet ^{
        Cost=Mini
        Name=Голографический планшет
        OnScriptActCode ^{
            00=[t_OnItemPickUp,t_OnItemHit,t_OnItemDestroy,t_OnStep|11]
            01=if(ScriptItemActionType(t_OnItemPickUp))
            01 ^{
                01=if(IsPlayer(ScriptItemActShip()))
                01 ^{
                    01=dword tziptzilot = GroupToShip(BlasterTziptzilot);
                    02=ReleaseItemFromScript(HoloTablet);
                    03=ScriptItemActParam(-1);
                    04=SetData(1, 0, tziptzilot);
                    05=SetData(MercCenterId * 2 + 1, 2, tziptzilot);
                    06=NoTalkToShip(tziptzilot, 0);
                    07=for(int i = 1; i <= 2; i = i + 1) ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('UselessItems.ShuMercsHQ_HoloTablet.SpawnTrancCollector'), 'tranc_num', i, 'cur_state', 'TrancsPlayerChaseState');
                    08=SetData(1, 3, tziptzilot);
                    09=FilmFlags(3, 1);
                    10=BuildOwnBase_Condition = 2;
                }
            }
            02=else if(ScriptItemActionType(t_OnStep))
            02 ^{
                01=tziptzilot = GroupToShip(BlasterTziptzilot);
                02=if(Dist(Player(), CurItem()) > 1200)
                02 ^{
                    01=ReleaseItemFromScript(HoloTablet);
                    02=SetData(3, 0, tziptzilot);
                    03=SetData(Id(StarPlanets(StarNearbyStars(ShipStar(tziptzilot), 65), 0)) * 2, 2, tziptzilot);
                    04=ChangeState('TziptzilotFlyAwayState', tziptzilot);
                    05=NoTalkToShip(tziptzilot, 0);
                    06=dword hook = ShipEqInSlot(tziptzilot, t_CargoHook);
                    07=if(ItemIsBroken(hook)) ItemDurability(hook, 10);
                    08=ShipSpecialBonuses(tziptzilot, bonHookRadius, 300);
                    09=ShipPicksItem(tziptzilot, CurItem(), 1);
                    10=for(i = 1; i <= 2; i = i + 1) ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('UselessItems.ShuMercsHQ_HoloTablet.SpawnTrancCollector'), 'tranc_num', i, 'cur_state', 'TrancsFlyAwayState');
                    11=for(i = 0; i < 2; i = i + 1)
                    11 ^{
                        01=dword tr = GroupShip(TrancsCollectors, i);
                        02=SetData(Id(StarNearbyStars(ShipStar(tziptzilot), 65)) - 1, 0, tr);
                        03=SetData(Id(tziptzilot), 1, tr);
                        04=SetData(0, 2, tr);
                        05=ShipSetBad(tr, tziptzilot);
                    }
                    12=BuildOwnBase_Condition = -BuildOwnBase_Condition;
                    13=if(ShipInNormalSpace(Player()))
                    13 ^{

                        }
                    }
                }
                03=else if(!ShipInNormalSpace(Player()) || ShipStar(Player()) != ScriptItemActObject1())
                03 ^{
                    01=ReleaseItemFromScript(HoloTablet);
                    02=SetData(3, 0, tziptzilot);
                    03=SetData(Id(StarPlanets(StarNearbyStars(ShipStar(tziptzilot), 65), 0)) * 2, 2, tziptzilot);
                    04=ChangeState('TziptzilotFlyAwayState', tziptzilot);
                    05=NoTalkToShip(tziptzilot, 0);

                    07=if(ItemIsBroken(hook)) ItemDurability(hook, 10);
                    08=ShipSpecialBonuses(tziptzilot, bonHookRadius, 300);
                    09=ShipPicksItem(tziptzilot, CurItem(), 1);
                    10=for(i = 1; i <= 2; i = i + 1) ExecuteCodeFromString(GenerateCodeStringFromBlock('UselessItems.ShuMercsHQ_HoloTablet.SpawnTrancCollector'), 'tranc_num', i, 'cur_state', 'TrancsFlyAwayState');
                    11=for(i = 0; i < 2; i = i + 1)

                        01=tr = GroupShip(TrancsCollectors, i);
                        02=SetData(Id(StarNearbyStars(ShipStar(tziptzilot), 65)) - 1, 0, tr);
                        03=SetData(Id(tziptzilot), 1, tr);
                        04=SetData(0, 2, tr);
                        05=ShipSetBad(tr, tziptzilot);
                    }
                    12=BuildOwnBase_Condition = -BuildOwnBase_Condition;
                }
                04=else if(~ScriptItemData(CurItem(), 1))
                04 ^{
                    01=if(CurTurn() >= ScriptItemData(CurItem(), 1))
                    01 ^{
                        01=ReleaseItemFromScript(HoloTablet);
                        02=SetData(3, 0, tziptzilot);
                        03=SetData(Id(StarPlanets(StarNearbyStars(ShipStar(tziptzilot), 65), 0)) * 2, 2, tziptzilot);

                    }

                    01 ^{
                        01=ItemDestroy(CurItem(), -1);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Owner=Peleng
        Size=1
        SpawnTrancCollector ^{
            01=unknown RoundTo = ImportedFunction('UtilityFunctions', 'RoundTo');
            02=unknown RndObject = ImportedFunction('UtilityFunctions', 'RndObject');
            03=unknown PortionInDiapason = ImportedFunction('UtilityFunctions', 'PortionInDiapason');
            04=unknown ShipJoinToScript = ImportedFunction('UtilityFunctions', 'ShipJoinToScript');
            05=dword tranc = BuyTranclucator(PlanetPirateClan(), 0);
            06=dword hull = ShipItems(tranc, 0);
            07=ItemSize(hull, max(ItemSize(hull), 125 * GalaxyTechLevel(), 300));
            08=ItemSize(hull, ItemSize(hull) + RndObject(-15, 15, tranc));
            09=HullHP(hull, 'To100');
            10=dword radar = CreateEquipment(t_Radar, RndObject(25, 35, tranc), GalaxyTechLevel(), PirateClan);
            11=AddItemToShip(tranc, radar);
            12=ItemIsInUse(radar, tranc, 1);
            13=NoDropItem(radar, 1);
            14=ShipSpecialBonuses(tranc, bonSlotRadar, 1);
            15=ShipSpecialBonuses(tranc, bonHull, GalaxyDiffLevels(7) / 10);
            16=ShipMoney(tranc, 50000000);
            17=for(int j = 1; j <= 9; j = j + 1) ShipRefit(tranc);
            18=ShipImproveItems(tranc, 12);
            19=ShipMoney(tranc, 0);
            20=int speed_limit = PortionInDiapason(GalaxyDiffLevels(), 50, 500, 450, 950);
            21=while(ShipSpeed(tranc) > speed_limit)
            22 ^{
                01=ShipSpecialBonuses(tranc, bonSpeed, ShipSpecialBonuses(tranc, bonSpeed) - 1);
                02=ShipCalcParam(tranc);
            }
            23=while(ShipSpeed(tranc) < speed_limit)
            24 ^{
                01=ShipSpecialBonuses(tranc, bonSpeed, ShipSpecialBonuses(tranc, bonSpeed) + 1);
                02=ShipCalcParam(tranc);
            }
            25=SetName(tranc, CT('Script.Mod_ShuMercs.QuestBeforePlayerOrgDialogs.TrancName' + tranc_num));
            26=if(PirateWin() != 3 && PirateWin() != 4) ShipOwner(tranc, PirateClan);
            27=else ShipOwner(tranc, People);
            28=ShipJoinToScript(tranc, 'Mod_ShuMercsHQ.TrancsCollectors.' + cur_state);
            29=SetData(tranc_num, 0, tranc);
            30=SetData(3, 1, tranc);
            31=int cost = RoundTo(max(1000000, 30 * GalaxyMoney(4, 4)), 100000, 0);
            32=SetData(cost, 3, tranc);
            33=if(IsScriptActive('Mod_EvoTranc'))
            34 ^{
                01=Chameleon(tranc, 'Ship.TranclucatorWhite');
                02=EquipmentImageName(tranc, 'ArtTranclucatorWhite');
            }
            35=ShipSetBad(tranc, Player());
            36=MakeShipEnterStar(tranc, ShipStar(Player()), StarNearbyStars(ShipStar(Player()), 60), 1);
        }
        Text=Обычный планшет, из тех, какие обычно продаются на любой мало-мальски развитой планете.
    }
